## 17. Zoho ERP Endpoint Inventory

### 17.1 What this inventory is, and what it is not

Every row below is transcribed from the Zoho ERP official OpenAPI bundle `openapi-all.zip`, sha256 `E95A039969F5C2FC2A44C47CFF77E41BACFF18957DCA1C870D48DDDA15C447F8`, downloaded 2026-08-05. The bundle contains **869 operations across 561 distinct paths and 163 distinct OAuth scopes, in 78 modules**. No endpoint, method, parameter or scope in this section was written from memory. **If a path is not in this inventory, it does not exist for the purposes of this build.**

Columns 1 to 17 and 25 to 27 are transcribed or mechanically derived from the specification. Columns 18 to 24 - Direction, Trigger, Sync frequency, Idempotency method, Reconciliation method, POC usage, Production usage - are **design decisions made by this blueprint**, not vendor statements. They tell the implementing agent how the CAPEX and CWIP Control Hub is to use each call. Where a design column would not fit a table cell, the cell carries the operative rule and the full text appears in the **Design policy** block immediately above the table.

Scope: the 21 modules relevant to CAPEX and CWIP control, comprising **%d of the 869 operations**. The remaining 57 modules - subscriptions, sales orders, invoices, manufacturing, payroll and the rest - are out of scope for this application and are not reproduced. Within the 21 modules every operation is listed, including operations the design does not use; those are marked `Not used` in both usage columns so that the reader can see they were considered and rejected rather than missed.

### 17.2 Base-path resolution and the `/erp/v1` exception

The ERP API is **not** uniformly `/erp/v3`. The bundle resolves **776 operations to `https://www.zohoapis.in/erp/v3` and 93 operations to `https://www.zohoapis.in/erp/v1`**, across 64 v3 modules and 14 v1 modules (finding OAS-01).

The 14 modules on `/erp/v1` are `departments`, `designations`, `employee-salary`, `employee-statutory`, `employees`, `expense-categories`, `expense-claims`, `expense-reports`, `income-tax-details`, `payruns`, `receipts`, `salary-revisions`, `trips` and `work-center-timings`.

Exactly one of those is in CAPEX scope: **`departments`**, which supplies the Department dimension on the CAPEX Project master (REQ-PRJ-011). Its rows are flagged **(/erp/v1)** in the Zoho ERP module column and its base-path cell reads `/erp/v1`.

Note the trap in the v1 list: `expense-categories`, `expense-claims` and `expense-reports` are on v1, while the `expenses` module used by this design is on v3. Three similarly named modules, two different base paths.

**Binding consequence for the implementer.** The Zoho Connection Profile entity must not hold a single `api_base_path` string. Base path and API version are per-module attributes of the API Endpoint Catalogue entity, resolved at call time. A connector that hard-codes `/erp/v3` fails on department sync with an HTTP 404 and no useful diagnostic. See section 14 Zoho ERP Connector Setup Module.

### 17.3 Contracts that apply to every row

| Contract | Value | Evidence |
|---|---|---|
| Data centre host | `https://www.zohoapis.in` (India). Token host `https://accounts.zoho.in` | "Note: For Zoho ERP (India), use https://accounts.zoho.in as {Accounts_URL} when generating tokens." [source: https://www.zoho.com/erp/api/v3/oauth/ — verified 2026-08-05] |
| Mandatory query parameter | `organization_id` on effectively every call in this inventory | OpenAPI bundle, per-row Required parameters column |
| Pagination parameters | `page`, `per_page` | "$ curl https://www.zohoapis.in/erp/v3/contacts?page=2&per_page=25" [source: https://www.zoho.com/erp/api/v3/pagination/ — verified 2026-08-05] |
| Pagination defaults | `page` defaults to 1 (73 occurrences in the bundle); `per_page` defaults to 200 (64 occurrences) or 10 (10 occurrences); documented maximum 200 | Finding OAS-08, machine-extracted from the bundle |
| Pagination response node | `page_context` | "Zoho ERP provides APIs to retrieve lists of contacts, plans and other resources - paginated to 200 items by default. The pagination information will be included in the list API response under the node name page_context." [source: https://www.zoho.com/erp/api/v3/pagination/ — verified 2026-08-05] |
| Rate limit | "You can make 100 requests per minute per organization." | [source: https://www.zoho.com/erp/api/v3/introduction/ — verified 2026-08-05] |
| Concurrency limit | "All plans - 10 concurrent calls (soft limit)"; "Trial plans - 5 concurrent calls (soft limit)" | [source: https://www.zoho.com/erp/api/v3/introduction/ — verified 2026-08-05] |
| OAuth scope grammar | "The format to define a scope is scope = service_name.scope_name.operation_type"; operation types CREATE, READ, UPDATE, DELETE, ALL | [source: https://www.zoho.com/erp/api/v3/oauth/ — verified 2026-08-05] |
| Refresh token | "A refresh token does not expire." "The maximum limit is 20 refresh tokens per user." The oldest is deleted when the limit is crossed, whether or not it is in use | [source: https://www.zoho.com/erp/api/v3/oauth/ — verified 2026-08-05] |
| Webhook provisioning | No webhook management API exists in the ERP REST surface. Webhooks are a workflow ACTION configured only in the Zoho ERP UI: method PUT, POST or DELETE with POST the default; payload Default Payload, x-www-form-urlencoded or Raw; secret token alphanumeric, 12 to 50 characters | [source: https://www.zoho.com/en-in/erp/help/automation/workflow-actions/webhooks.html — verified 2026-08-05] |

**Rate-limit sizing.** With 100 requests per minute per organisation and a soft concurrency ceiling of 10, the connector runs a single token-bucket limiter shared by every sync job rather than a limiter per job. A 15-minute purchase-order delta poll at `per_page=200` costs one request per 200 changed purchase orders, so the scheduled load is trivial and the budget exists for the event-driven single reads that dominate real traffic. The binding constraint is not throughput but the full-refresh modules: a nightly fixed-asset refresh and a nightly vendor refresh are the two jobs whose cost grows with the estate. See section 25 API and Event Architecture for the retry, backoff and dead-letter design.

### 17.4 Column definitions

| # | Column | Source | Meaning |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | Business object | Specification | The Zoho business object the operation belongs to. |
| 2 | Zoho ERP module | Specification | Module name as it appears in the bundle. `/erp/v1` modules are flagged. |
| 3 | Official documentation reference | Specification plus doc sweep | Corroborating Zoho documentation page where the sweep found one. `None` means the operation is evidenced by the bundle alone, which is still vendor-authoritative. |
| 4 | API base path | Specification | Resolved server for this module. |
| 5 | Endpoint | Specification | Path, relative to the base path. |
| 6 | HTTP method | Specification | Verb. |
| 7 | Required OAuth scope | Specification | Scope string the operation declares. |
| 8 | Required parameters | Specification | Parameters the specification marks required. |
| 9 | Required body fields | Specification | Body schema fields, and line-item schema fields where a line array exists. |
| 10 | Response identifiers | Derived from specification | Identifiers the Control Hub must persist from the response, derived from path placeholders, the module primary key and the line-item schema. |
| 11 | Pagination support | Specification | Whether `page` and `per_page` are accepted. |
| 12 | Filter support | Specification | Filter and sort parameters accepted. |
| 13 | Custom-field support | Specification | Whether custom-field VALUES can be set. **No API creates a custom-field DEFINITION** (finding OAS-06). |
| 14 | Approval action support | Derived from specification | Approval endpoints present anywhere in the module. |
| 15 | Attachment support | Derived from specification | Attachment endpoint present anywhere in the module. |
| 16 | Last-modified field | Derived from specification | Whether the module list accepts `last_modified_time`, which decides incremental versus full-refresh sync. |
| 17 | Status field | Derived from specification | Status transition endpoints, or a status list filter. |
| 18 | Direction | **Design decision** | Which way data moves for this call in this design. |
| 19 | Trigger | **Design decision** | What causes the call. |
| 20 | Sync frequency | **Design decision** | How often. |
| 21 | Idempotency method | **Design decision** | How a replay is made safe. Read methods are safe by definition. |
| 22 | Reconciliation method | **Design decision** | How the two sides are proved equal. Stated in brief here, in full in the Design policy block above each table, and in the round in section 27 Integration Reconciliation Design. |
| 23 | POC usage | **Design decision** | Whether the POC calls it, and at which numbered POC step. |
| 24 | Production usage | **Design decision** | Whether production calls it. |
| 25 | Verified date | Specification | 2026-08-05 on every row. |
| 26 | Verification status | Specification | CONFIRMED-BY-VENDOR-SPECIFICATION, with the bundle citation. |
| 27 | Limitation | Specification and findings | Row-specific constraint. Module-wide limitations appear in the Specification note above each table. |

### 17.5 Module index

| # | Module | API version | Operations | Used by the design | Role in CAPEX and CWIP control | Incremental sync |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | `organizations` | v3 | 4 | 4 | Connector bootstrap. | **No - full refresh** |
| 2 | `users` | v3 | 9 | 0 | Resolves the Zoho user who created or approved a Zoho-side transaction, so the Control Hub audit trail names a person rather than an opaque identifier. | **No - full refresh** |
| 3 | `departments` | **v1** | 5 | 4 | Supplies the Department dimension on the CAPEX Project master (REQ-PRJ-011). | **No - full refresh** |
| 4 | `contacts` | v3 | 21 | 7 | Vendor master behind every Purchase Order Reference and Vendor Bill Reference. | **No - full refresh** |
| 5 | `items` | v3 | 10 | 9 | Item master for Purchase Request lines and PO lines. | **No - full refresh** |
| 6 | `locations` | v3 | 8 | 6 | Plant and Location dimension on the CAPEX Project master (REQ-PRJ-010) and on PO, bill and journal lines via `location_id`. | **No - full refresh** |
| 7 | `projects` | v3 | 24 | 12 | The Zoho-side anchor for a CAPEX Project. | **No - full refresh** |
| 8 | `tasks` | v3 | 17 | 6 | The only Zoho ERP object exposing `parent_id`, and therefore the only candidate for mirroring a WBS Element tree into Zoho. | **No - full refresh** |
| 9 | `taxes` | v3 | 15 | 6 | Tax master needed to build a compliant PO or bill payload and to value an ingested line net of tax when computing CWIP. | Yes - `last_modified_time` |
| 10 | `currency` | v3 | 10 | 8 | Base-currency and exchange-rate discovery. | **No - full refresh** |
| 11 | `chart-of-accounts` | v3 | 12 | 9 | Supplies the CWIP general ledger accounts (REQ-PRJ-015, REQ-INT-007). | Yes - `last_modified_time` |
| 12 | `reporting-tags` | v3 | 14 | 13 | The primary alternative carrier for the CAPEX code and Budget Head at line level, alongside `project_id` and `item_custom_fields`. | **No - full refresh** |
| 13 | `custom-modules` | v3 | 12 | 9 | Optional. | **No - full refresh** |
| 14 | `purchase-order` | v3 | 26 | 17 | The commitment source. | Yes - `last_modified_time` |
| 15 | `purchasereceives` | v3 | 4 | 3 | Goods receipt against a purchase order (REQ-GRN-001). | **No - full refresh** |
| 16 | `bills` | v3 | 22 | 12 | The actual-cost source. | Yes - `last_modified_time` |
| 17 | `vendor-credits` | v3 | 21 | 10 | Reduces Actual CWIP when capital goods are returned or a bill is credited. | Yes - `last_modified_time` |
| 18 | `vendor-payments` | v3 | 14 | 5 | Identifies capital advances. | Yes - `last_modified_time` |
| 19 | `expenses` | v3 | 11 | 6 | Second actual-cost path. | **No - full refresh** |
| 20 | `journals` | v3 | 31 | 14 | The capitalisation instrument. | Yes - `last_modified_time` |
| 21 | `fixed-assets` | v3 | 18 | 12 | The capitalisation target. | **No - full refresh** |

**7 of the 21 modules in scope expose `last_modified_time` on their list endpoint** (`taxes`, `chart-of-accounts`, `purchase-order`, `bills`, `vendor-credits`, `vendor-payments`, `journals`). The other 14 are full refresh or date-window scan, and two of those - `purchasereceives` and `fixed-assets` - carry the two hardest constraints in the whole integration. That is not a tuning problem to be optimised away later; it is a design constraint that shapes section 27 Integration Reconciliation Design and the nightly job schedule.

### 17.6 The inventory

#### 17.6.1 Organizations

**Purpose in this design.** Connector bootstrap. The organisation list is the first authenticated call the setup wizard makes, and the chosen `organization_id` is pinned into the Zoho Connection Profile and sent on every later request.

**Specification note.** `organization_id` is a required query parameter on effectively every other endpoint in this inventory, so this module gates the entire connector. The OAuth scope printed for the organisation call is `ERP.settings.READ` [source: https://www.zoho.com/erp/api/v3/introduction/ — verified 2026-08-05], which is also the broadest read scope in the ERP family - see section 15 OAuth, Credential and Scope Management for the scope-minimisation consequence.

**Design policy.**

- **Reconciliation in full.** The `organization_id` held in Zoho Connection Profile is compared with the discovered organisation list on every health check. A mismatch - organisation renamed, deactivated or the credential re-consented against a different org - raises a Reconciliation Log entry and puts the profile into status Integration Failed rather than silently syncing against the wrong books.
- **Why write is blocked.** Organisation settings are an Atha Group administrator activity in the Zoho ERP UI. The Control Hub has no business changing base currency or fiscal year, and the allow-list makes that structural rather than a matter of discipline.

**Module facts.** API version `v3`. Base path `https://www.zohoapis.in/erp/v3`. 4 operations, 4 used by the design. Primary identifier `organization_id`. Last-modified: **None - full refresh**. Approval actions: absent. Attachments: absent. Every row below is [source: Zoho ERP OpenAPI bundle openapi-all.zip, sha256 E95A0399... — verified 2026-08-05]

#### 17.6.2 Users

**Purpose in this design.** Resolves the Zoho user who created or approved a Zoho-side transaction, so the Control Hub audit trail names a person rather than an opaque identifier.

**Specification note.** `filter_by` on the user list accepts `Status.All`, `Status.Active`, `Status.Inactive`, `Status.Invited` and `Status.Deleted` [source: https://www.zoho.com/erp/api/v3/users/ — verified 2026-08-05]. There is no last-modified filter, so this module is full-refresh. `GET /users/me` is the cheapest possible token-liveness probe and is what the connection health check should call.

**Design policy.**

- **Reconciliation in full.** An inbound Purchase Order Reference or Vendor Bill Reference quoting a Zoho user id that does not resolve to a cached user raises a Reconciliation Log entry of type UNKNOWN-ACTOR. The transaction is still ingested - a missing display name must never block a commitment figure - but the audit trail shows the raw id until the next user sync resolves it.
- **Why write is blocked.** User administration and licence assignment stay in Zoho ERP. `POST /users` and `POST /users/{user_id}/invite` are present in the specification and deliberately not enabled.

**Module facts.** API version `v3`. Base path `https://www.zohoapis.in/erp/v3`. 9 operations, 0 used by the design. Primary identifier `user_id`. Last-modified: **None - full refresh**. #### 17.6.3 Departments - **served from `/erp/v1`, not `/erp/v3`**

**Purpose in this design.** Supplies the Department dimension on the CAPEX Project master (REQ-PRJ-011).

**Specification note.** **API-VERSION EXCEPTION.** This module is served from `https://www.zohoapis.in/erp/v1`. It is one of the 14 modules on the v1 base path (finding OAS-01) and the only one of those 14 in CAPEX scope. Its OAuth scope family is `ERP.department.*`, singular, and does not appear on the published OAuth documentation page. The connection profile therefore cannot hold one API base path - base path resolves per module. See section 14 Zoho ERP Connector Setup Module.

**Design policy.**

- **Reconciliation in full.** An unresolved department code blocks CAPEX Project approval with an explicit validation message. It never falls back to a default department, because a silently defaulted dimension corrupts every dashboard slice built on it.
- **Base path.** Every row in this table resolves to `/erp/v1`. A connector that composes the URL from a single configured base path will return HTTP 404 here with no useful diagnostic.

**Module facts.** API version `v1`. Base path `https://www.zohoapis.in/erp/v1`. 5 operations, 4 used by the design. Primary identifier `department_id`. Last-modified: **None - full refresh**. #### 17.6.4 Contacts (vendors)

**Purpose in this design.** Vendor master behind every Purchase Order Reference and Vendor Bill Reference.

**Specification note.** Contact custom fields are index-capped: `index integer Index of the custom field. It can hold any value from 1 to 10.` [source: https://www.zoho.com/erp/api/v3/contacts/ — verified 2026-08-05]. There is no last-modified filter on the contact list, so vendor sync is full refresh.

**Design policy.**

- **Reconciliation in full.** A nightly count-and-hash comparison of active vendors detects drift cheaply. Any vendor carrying an open PO commitment is additionally re-read individually, because a vendor made inactive while holding open capital commitments is a control event, not a master-data event.
- **Source of truth.** Zoho ERP owns vendor master. The Control Hub holds a read-only mirror. Enabling outbound vendor creation would put two systems in charge of one record and is recommended against - see section 13 Source-of-Truth Matrix.

**Module facts.** API version `v3`. Base path `https://www.zohoapis.in/erp/v3`. 21 operations, 7 used by the design. Primary identifier `contact_id`. Last-modified: **None - full refresh**. #### 17.6.5 Items

**Purpose in this design.** Item master for Purchase Request lines and PO lines. Carries the line-level `item_custom_fields` array that can hold a CAPEX or WBS code.

**Specification note.** Item custom-field VALUES can be written; the custom-field DEFINITION cannot (finding OAS-06). `GET /itemdetails` returns an expanded item projection and is the cheaper call when the picker needs stock and account data together.

**Design policy.**

- **Reconciliation in full.** An item id quoted on an inbound PO line that does not resolve raises a Reconciliation Log entry. The line is still ingested with `item_id` retained and `item_name` null, because a missing description must never suppress a commitment.
- **Path irregularity.** `PUT /item/{item_id}/customfields` is singular while the rest of the module is plural `/items`. That is transcribed from the bundle, not a typo in this table.

**Module facts.** API version `v3`. Base path `https://www.zohoapis.in/erp/v3`. 10 operations, 9 used by the design. Primary identifier `item_id`. Last-modified: **None - full refresh**. Approval actions: absent. Attachments: absent. Every row below is [source: Zoho ERP OpenAPI bundle openapi-all.zip, sha256 E95A0399... — verified 2026-08-05]

#### 17.6.6 Locations

**Purpose in this design.** Plant and Location dimension on the CAPEX Project master (REQ-PRJ-010) and on PO, bill and journal lines via `location_id`.

**Specification note.** The location list carries neither pagination parameters nor a last-modified filter in the specification, so it is read whole on each sync.

**Design policy.**

- **Reconciliation in full.** Unresolved location ids raise a Reconciliation Log entry. For a greenfield plant the location list is a handful of rows, so full refresh costs one request.
- **Prerequisite.** `POST /settings/locations/enable` switches multi-location on for the organisation. If locations were never enabled, `location_id` is absent from every line and the plant dimension is unavailable - the setup wizard must probe for this and say so, rather than showing an empty picklist.

**Module facts.** API version `v3`. Base path `https://www.zohoapis.in/erp/v3`. 8 operations, 6 used by the design. Primary identifier `location_id`. Last-modified: **None - full refresh**. #### 17.6.7 Projects

**Purpose in this design.** The Zoho-side anchor for a CAPEX Project. `project_id` is the only structured coding field available simultaneously on a PO line, a bill line and a journal line, which makes it the primary carrier of the CAPEX code.

**Specification note.** **Zoho ERP projects are flat.** The project body carries only `budget_amount`, `budget_type` and `cost_budget_amount` - scalars, with no budget-by-head object (finding OAS-05). Hierarchy exists only under Tasks, via `parent_id`. The WBS Element tree and the Budget Head register therefore stay owned by the Control Hub. There is no last-modified filter on the project list.

**Design policy.**

- **Idempotency in full.** `PUT /projects` accepts `X-Unique-Identifier-Key`, `X-Unique-Identifier-Value` and `X-Upsert`, which gives a true upsert keyed on a custom field. That is the preferred production mechanism and it removes the create-versus-update decision from the connector entirely. Where the upsert headers are not available, create is preceded by a lookup on the CAPEX code prefix carried in `project_name`.
- **Reconciliation in full.** Every Control Hub CAPEX Project in status Approved or later must have exactly one mapped Zoho `project_id` in Zoho Organisation Mapping. Orphans in either direction - a Control Hub project with no mirror, or a Zoho project carrying a CAPEX prefix with no Control Hub parent - raise a Reconciliation Log entry.
- **What is deliberately not mirrored.** Budget. The Zoho project's `budget_amount` is a single scalar and cannot represent a budget-head breakdown, so writing to it would create a second, wrong version of the truth.

**Module facts.** API version `v3`. Base path `https://www.zohoapis.in/erp/v3`. 24 operations, 12 used by the design. Primary identifier `project_id`. Last-modified: **None - full refresh**. Approval actions: absent. Attachments: absent. Every row below is [source: Zoho ERP OpenAPI bundle openapi-all.zip, sha256 E95A0399... — verified 2026-08-05]

#### 17.6.8 Tasks

**Purpose in this design.** The only Zoho ERP object exposing `parent_id`, and therefore the only candidate for mirroring a WBS Element tree into Zoho. Recommended OFF for the POC.

**Specification note.** Task custom fields carry a `cf_` API name, for example `"label": "Project Code", "api_name": "cf_project_code"` [source: https://www.zoho.com/erp/api/v3/tasks/ — verified 2026-08-05]. The WBS layer itself is a SCOPE ADDITION (CONF-02): the client PDF contains no WBS hierarchy at all, only project plus budget head.

**Design policy.**

- **Why this module is off.** A Zoho task carries no budget, no commitment and no availability control. Mirroring the WBS into tasks buys structure without control, at the cost of a second hierarchy to keep in step. The blueprint records it as an option and recommends against it for the POC.
- **If it is ever enabled.** Task custom fields carry a `cf_` API name, so the WBS code would live in `cf_wbs_code` and upsert would key on it. The custom-field definition would still have to be created manually first (finding OAS-06).

**Module facts.** API version `v3`. Base path `https://www.zohoapis.in/erp/v3`. 17 operations, 6 used by the design. Primary identifier `task_id`. Last-modified: **None - full refresh**. Approval actions: absent. Attachments: present. Every row below is [source: Zoho ERP OpenAPI bundle openapi-all.zip, sha256 E95A0399... — verified 2026-08-05]

#### 17.6.9 Taxes

**Purpose in this design.** Tax master needed to build a compliant PO or bill payload and to value an ingested line net of tax when computing CWIP.

**Specification note.** Whether recoverable input tax is excluded from CWIP is an accounting decision, not an API one - see section 23 Accounting Design. The API supplies the tax breakdown; the policy decides what to capitalise.

**Design policy.**

- **Reconciliation in full.** A tax id quoted on an inbound line that does not resolve holds that line at Reconciliation Pending rather than valuing it gross, because silently treating an unknown tax as zero would overstate CWIP.
- **Cursor.** This is one of only eight modules in scope whose list exposes `last_modified_time`, so it supports a genuine incremental cursor. The cursor value lives in Sync Cursor and is advanced only after the page is durably persisted.

**Module facts.** API version `v3`. Base path `https://www.zohoapis.in/erp/v3`. 15 operations, 6 used by the design. Primary identifier `tax_id`. Last-modified: `last_modified_time` - incremental. #### 17.6.10 Currency

**Purpose in this design.** Base-currency and exchange-rate discovery. Atha Group CAPEX is INR-denominated, but imported plant and machinery is commonly ordered in a foreign currency.

**Specification note.** The FX policy - transaction-date rate versus period rate, and whether a rate movement restates a commitment - is an open accounting decision recorded in section 23 Accounting Design. The API settles none of it.

**Design policy.**

- **Reconciliation in full.** A foreign-currency PO or bill ingested without an `exchange_rate` is held at Reconciliation Pending, not valued at 1:1. An implicit 1:1 on an imported machinery PO is a seven-figure error, and it is silent.
- **Presentation.** Budget, commitment, actual and available figures are held and displayed in the organisation base currency throughout the Control Hub. The transaction currency is retained on the line for drill-down only.

**Module facts.** API version `v3`. Base path `https://www.zohoapis.in/erp/v3`. 10 operations, 8 used by the design. Primary identifier `currency_id`. Last-modified: **None - full refresh**. #### 17.6.11 Chart of Accounts

**Purpose in this design.** Supplies the CWIP general ledger accounts (REQ-PRJ-015, REQ-INT-007). `account_id` is the field that decides whether an ingested line is CWIP-eligible at all.

**Specification note.** The account list exposes `last_modified_time`, so this module supports incremental sync. `GET /chartofaccounts/accounttransactions` returns posted transactions per account and is the independent check used by the reconciliation in section 27 - it lets the Control Hub prove its CWIP total against the ledger rather than against its own ingestion history.

**Design policy.**

- **Reconciliation in full.** The CWIP account allow-list held in Sync Configuration is re-validated nightly. An account made inactive while open CWIP is posted against it raises a Reconciliation Log entry at severity high - that is a finance-side change with a direct effect on a controlled balance.
- **Why this module decides the numbers.** Only lines whose `account_id` is in the CWIP allow-list increase Actual CWIP. That single mechanical filter is what keeps revenue expenditure out of the CAPEX position, and it is why GL account creation must never be automated from the Control Hub. See section 22 Budget and Commitment Calculation Logic.

**Module facts.** API version `v3`. Base path `https://www.zohoapis.in/erp/v3`. 12 operations, 9 used by the design. Primary identifier `account_id`. Last-modified: `last_modified_time` - incremental. Approval actions: absent. Attachments: absent. #### 17.6.12 Reporting Tags

**Purpose in this design.** The primary alternative carrier for the CAPEX code and Budget Head at line level, alongside `project_id` and `item_custom_fields`.

**Specification note.** Reporting tags are applicable at line level: `preference string Preference type. Currently, only 'transaction' and 'line_item' is supported for this node.` [source: https://www.zoho.com/erp/api/v3/reporting-tags/ — verified 2026-08-05]. Taggable entities are `Sales - sales, Purchases - purchases, Journal - journals, Inventory Adjustment - inventory_adjustment` [source: https://www.zoho.com/erp/api/v3/reporting-tags/ — verified 2026-08-05]. **Note what that list does not contain: purchase receives.** Two endpoints in this module are published with a raw regular expression in the path, `(\d+)`, rather than a named parameter; the connector must substitute the numeric option id positionally.

**Design policy.**

- **Reconciliation in full.** Every active Budget Head must have exactly one active Zoho tag option, and every active tag option in the CAPEX tag must map to a Budget Head. Both directions are checked nightly; drift raises a Reconciliation Log entry. An orphaned tag option is worse than a missing one, because transactions can be coded to it and then vanish from every Control Hub report.
- **Why an id, not a name.** Tag options can be renamed in the Zoho ERP UI. All Control Hub references are by `tag_option_id`, so a rename is cosmetic rather than a break.

**Module facts.** API version `v3`. Base path `https://www.zohoapis.in/erp/v3`. 14 operations, 13 used by the design. Primary identifier `tag_id`. Last-modified: **None - full refresh**. Approval actions: absent. Attachments: absent. Every row below is [source: Zoho ERP OpenAPI bundle openapi-all.zip, sha256 E95A0399... — verified 2026-08-05]

#### 17.6.13 Custom Modules

**Purpose in this design.** Optional. Would allow a Control Hub record - a Purchase Request, for instance - to be stored inside Zoho ERP rather than only in the Control Hub. Recommended OFF.

**Specification note.** The OAuth scope is `ERP.custommodules.ALL` [source: https://www.zoho.com/erp/api/v3/custom-modules/ — verified 2026-08-05], and it does not appear on the published OAuth page. Record paths are `/{module_name}` and `/{module_name}/{module_id}`, so the module API name discovered from `GET /settings/modules` becomes part of the URL.

**Design policy.**

- **Why this module is off.** The scope is `ERP.custommodules.ALL` - an ALL-only scope with no READ variant. Enabling custom modules forfeits read-only least privilege across that whole surface, in exchange for storing a record the Control Hub already owns. That trade is not worth making for the POC.
- **What it does not solve.** A custom module is not a custom field. `GET /settings/modules` lists module definitions, but there is still no API that creates a custom FIELD definition (finding OAS-06). Reading the two as interchangeable is the most likely misreading of this inventory.

**Module facts.** API version `v3`. Base path `https://www.zohoapis.in/erp/v3`. 12 operations, 9 used by the design. Primary identifier `module_id`. Last-modified: **None - full refresh**. #### 17.6.14 Purchase Order

**Purpose in this design.** The commitment source. Every open PO line coded to a CAPEX Project and Budget Head becomes an Open PO Commitment in the Commitment Ledger (REQ-PO-001, REQ-BUD-002).

**Specification note.** **The enabling positive of the whole design (finding OAS-04).** A PO LINE carries `project_id`, `tags`, `item_custom_fields`, `account_id`, `location_id` and `line_item_category`. There is no field named capex, wbs, budget or cost centre anywhere in the schema, so the CAPEX and Budget Head code must ride on a line reporting tag option, a line `item_custom_field`, or the line `project_id` - the field-mapping workbench in section 19 chooses which. `line_item_category` allows `line_item`, `header` and `subtotal`, which is presentation-only grouping inside the item table; a header row is not a parent node and carries no id relationship to the lines beneath it, so **it cannot be used as a WBS roll-up** [source: https://www.zoho.com/erp/api/v3/purchase-order/ — verified 2026-08-05].

**Design policy.**

- **Reconciliation in full.** Three legs, run nightly and on demand. (1) Every open Zoho PO line coded to a CAPEX Project has a Commitment Ledger row. (2) Every Commitment Ledger row has a live Zoho PO line. (3) Open commitment summed per Budget Head, recomputed from Zoho, equals the stored Budget Ledger figure. Any break raises a Reconciliation Log entry and sets the affected Budget Head to Reconciliation Pending - the availability check then refuses to approve against a figure it cannot prove.
- **Idempotency in full.** The Control Hub Purchase Request number is written to `reference_number` on create. Before POST the connector issues `GET /purchaseorders?reference_number=<PR number>`; a hit means the PO already exists, the create is skipped, and the returned `purchaseorder_id` is bound into Purchase Order Reference. `ignore_auto_number_generation` is left false so that Zoho ERP continues to own the PO number series and no two systems mint numbers.
- **Amendment handling.** REQ-PO-008 requires revalidation of the incremental value on amendment. The delta poll detects the changed `last_modified_time`, the connector re-reads the full PO, recomputes line-level commitment, and the difference - not the new total - is what the availability check evaluates. See section 22 Budget and Commitment Calculation Logic.
- **Cancellation handling.** REQ-PO-009 requires release of unused commitment. `POST /purchaseorders/{purchaseorder_id}/status/cancelled` is the Zoho-side transition; on ingesting status `cancelled` the Control Hub closes the Commitment Ledger rows for that PO at their unbilled balance.

**Module facts.** API version `v3`. Base path `https://www.zohoapis.in/erp/v3`. 26 operations, 17 used by the design. Primary identifier `purchaseorder_id`. Last-modified: `last_modified_time` - incremental. Approval actions: present. Attachments: present. Every row below is [source: Zoho ERP OpenAPI bundle openapi-all.zip, sha256 E95A0399... — verified 2026-08-05]

#### 17.6.15 Purchase Receives (GRN)

**Purpose in this design.** Goods receipt against a purchase order (REQ-GRN-001). Received-but-unbilled exposure has to be reconstructed by the Control Hub, because the API will not yield it.

**Specification note.** **The hardest documented negative in this inventory (finding OAS-03).** The module exposes exactly four operations - POST, and GET, PUT, DELETE by id. There is **no list endpoint and no search endpoint**. A receipt LINE carries only `description`, `item_id`, `item_order`, `line_item_id`, `name`, `quantity` and `unit`: **no `purchaseorder_item_id`, no `project_id`, no `tags`, no custom fields**. Do not confuse this leg with the PO-to-bill leg, which does work - bill lines carry `purchaseorder_item_id`.

**Design policy.**

- **Idempotency in full, and why it is not optional here.** There is no list endpoint. A `purchasereceive_id` that is not persisted at the moment of creation is permanently unrecoverable through the API, and a blind retry after a timeout creates a duplicate receipt that can never be found and reconciled. The connector therefore writes the id inside the same database transaction as the local GRN, and treats a network timeout as status UNKNOWN - routed to Dead-Letter Record for human resolution - rather than as a failure to retry.
- **Reconciliation in full.** Per PO line, the received quantity held in the Control Hub is compared with the PO line position on every PO re-read. Divergence raises a Reconciliation Log entry of type GRN-DRIFT. A receipt created directly in the Zoho ERP UI is invisible to the Control Hub until it surfaces in the PO position or in a bill - that is a stated, unavoidable blind spot, not a defect in the connector.
- **What this costs the client.** Alert REQ-ALT-005, pending GRN and billing, depends entirely on the Control Hub-side reconstruction. If Atha Group intends receipts to be raised by storekeepers in the Zoho ERP UI rather than in the Control Hub, that alert degrades to PO-position inference and its accuracy should be stated to the client before build, not after.

**Module facts.** API version `v3`. Base path `https://www.zohoapis.in/erp/v3`. 4 operations, 3 used by the design. Primary identifier `purchasereceive_id`. Last-modified: **None - full refresh**. #### 17.6.16 Bills

**Purpose in this design.** The actual-cost source. A posted bill line hitting a CWIP account converts Open PO Commitment into Actual CWIP without double counting (REQ-CWIP-001, REQ-BILL-002, REQ-PO-003).

**Specification note.** **Bill LINES carry `purchaseorder_item_id`, so the PO-to-bill leg of line-level matching works** (finding OAS-03). Bill lines also carry `project_id`, `tags`, `item_custom_fields`, `account_id`, `line_item_id` and `location_id`. The bill **HEADER does not carry `project_id`** (finding OAS-10): every WBS-to-transaction mapping must therefore be defined at LINE level, never header. That is a binding rule for the field-mapping workbench in section 19.

**Design policy.**

- **Reconciliation in full.** For each PO line: the sum of billed quantity and value across all bill lines carrying that `purchaseorder_item_id` must equal the Actual Ledger position, and Open PO Commitment must equal PO line value less that sum, floored at zero. A negative residual - over-billing against a PO line - or a bill line whose `purchaseorder_item_id` does not resolve to a known PO line, raises a Reconciliation Log entry. This is the mechanical implementation of REQ-PO-003, no double counting.
- **Bill lines with no PO behind them.** A CWIP-coded bill line carrying no `purchaseorder_item_id` is legitimate - direct capital cost without a purchase order - and increases Actual CWIP with no commitment to release. It is flagged in the reconciliation report as UNCOMMITTED-ACTUAL so that finance can see how much capital cost bypassed the PO control, which is itself a control metric.
- **Void handling.** `POST /bills/{bill_id}/status/void` reverses the actual. On ingesting status `void` the Control Hub reverses the Actual Ledger rows and restores the commitment that the bill had consumed - it does not delete the original rows, because REQ-SEC-001 requires the trail to survive.

**Module facts.** API version `v3`. Base path `https://www.zohoapis.in/erp/v3`. 22 operations, 12 used by the design. Primary identifier `bill_id`. Last-modified: `last_modified_time` - incremental. #### 17.6.17 Vendor Credits (debit notes)

**Purpose in this design.** Reduces Actual CWIP when capital goods are returned or a bill is credited. Without these, CWIP overstates and the error is carried forward into the fixed asset register at capitalisation.

**Specification note.** Vendor credit lines carry line-level coding on the same pattern as bill lines. The module also exposes refund endpoints, which move cash rather than cost and are excluded from the CAPEX position.

**Design policy.**

- **Reconciliation in full.** Every vendor credit line coded to a CAPEX Project must have a matching negative Actual Ledger row, and the signed sum of bill lines and credit lines per Budget Head must equal the Actual CWIP figure in Budget Ledger. A credit that is ingested but not applied to a specific bill still reduces the project position; which PO line it relieves is a separate, best-effort match.
- **Scope naming trap.** The OAuth scope family is `ERP.debitnotes.*` while the module and paths are `vendorcredits`. A scope-validation screen that derives the expected scope from the module name will report a granted scope as missing.

**Module facts.** API version `v3`. Base path `https://www.zohoapis.in/erp/v3`. 21 operations, 10 used by the design. Primary identifier `vendor_credit_id`. Last-modified: `last_modified_time` - incremental. Approval actions: present. Attachments: absent. #### 17.6.18 Vendor Payments

**Purpose in this design.** Identifies capital advances. An advance paid against a capital PO is cash out with no CWIP yet, and Atha Group finance will expect to see it as exposure.

**Specification note.** **There is no `is_advance` flag and no dedicated advance endpoint (finding OAS-11).** The module exposes eight paths, none advance-specific. An advance is simply a vendor payment whose `bills[]` allocation array is empty.

**Design policy.**

- **Derivation rule, and it is a recommendation not a requirement.** A payment is treated as a capital advance when its `bills[]` allocation array is empty AND the vendor has at least one open PO coded to a CAPEX Project. Provenance: DERIVED-RECOMMENDATION. There is no `is_advance` flag to appeal to.
- **Open accounting question.** Whether a capital advance consumes Available Budget is not settled by the client document. The PDF's control equation counts commitment and actual only. An advance against an already-committed PO would be double counted if it were added, so the blueprint's default is that advances are DISPLAYED as exposure but NOT subtracted in the availability check - see section 22 Budget and Commitment Calculation Logic, and confirm with the client.
- **Why write is blocked.** Payment execution stays entirely in Zoho ERP. No design consideration justifies giving a budget-control application the ability to move money.

**Module facts.** API version `v3`. Base path `https://www.zohoapis.in/erp/v3`. 14 operations, 5 used by the design. Primary identifier `payment_id`. Last-modified: `last_modified_time` - incremental. Approval actions: absent. Attachments: absent. #### 17.6.19 Expenses

**Purpose in this design.** Second actual-cost path. Freight, site expenses and consultancy booked without a purchase order reach CWIP through expenses rather than bills, and Consultancy and Other is a live budget head in the client data (REQ-BUD-012).

**Specification note.** The expense list exposes no `last_modified_time` filter, so incremental sync is unavailable here and the connector must accept the re-read cost of a date-window scan.

**Design policy.**

- **Reconciliation in full.** Expense lines whose `account_id` is in the CWIP allow-list and which carry a CAPEX Project code must each have an Actual Ledger row. Because the list has no last-modified cursor, reconciliation re-scans a rolling window - default 45 days - rather than trusting a cursor it does not have. A back-dated expense outside that window is caught by the monthly full comparison, not the hourly scan.
- **Control consequence.** An expense booked to a CWIP account with no PO behind it produces Actual CWIP with no prior commitment. That is legitimate but it means Available Budget can fall without any commitment ever having been raised. The dashboard narrative in section 34 must explain this rather than present it as an anomaly, and it is a second contributor to the UNCOMMITTED-ACTUAL metric.

**Module facts.** API version `v3`. Base path `https://www.zohoapis.in/erp/v3`. 11 operations, 6 used by the design. Primary identifier `expense_id`. Last-modified: **None - full refresh**. Approval actions: absent. Attachments: present. Every row below is [source: Zoho ERP OpenAPI bundle openapi-all.zip, sha256 E95A0399... — verified 2026-08-05]

#### 17.6.20 Journals

**Purpose in this design.** The capitalisation instrument. The CWIP-to-fixed-asset transfer is posted as a journal whose lines are WBS-coded (REQ-CAP-001).

**Specification note.** **Journal LINES carry `project_id` and `tags` (finding OAS-10)**, which is what makes traceable capitalisation possible at all. Journal header custom fields also exist [source: https://www.zoho.com/erp/api/v3/journals/ — verified 2026-08-05]. The module also carries recurring-journal endpoints, listed here for completeness and not used by the CAPEX design.

**Design policy.**

- **Idempotency in full, and the two-phase post.** `reference_number` carries the Control Hub Capitalisation Request number, and a pre-flight `GET /journals?reference_number=<CAP number>` guards the create. The journal is created in DRAFT and only then published with `POST /journals/{journal_id}/status/publish`. A partial failure therefore leaves an unposted draft that a human can inspect and delete, rather than a wrong entry in a live ledger. This two-phase pattern is mandatory for every outbound journal.
- **Reconciliation in full.** Every Capitalisation Request in status Capitalised must have exactly one published Zoho journal, and the journal's CWIP-side total must equal the allocated CWIP amount to the paisa - no tolerance. Separately, any manual journal touching a CWIP account that the Control Hub did not originate raises a Reconciliation Log entry at severity high: that is an out-of-band change to a controlled balance and it must reach a human the same day.
- **Reversal.** `POST /journals/{journal_id}/reverse` is the sanctioned correction path for a capitalisation posted in error. Deletion is not enabled anywhere in this connector.

**Module facts.** API version `v3`. Base path `https://www.zohoapis.in/erp/v3`. 31 operations, 14 used by the design. Primary identifier `journal_id`. Last-modified: `last_modified_time` - incremental. Approval actions: present. Attachments: present. #### 17.6.21 Fixed Assets

**Purpose in this design.** The capitalisation target. Accumulated CWIP is transferred to one or more fixed assets at project completion (REQ-CAP-001, REQ-CAP-003).

**Specification note.** **Two hard negatives (finding OAS-09).** First, the create body contains no field linking the asset to a source project, purchase order or bill - the only date-like linkage field is `asset_purchase_date`. Second, the asset list exposes only `filter_by`, `sort_column`, `sort_order`, `page` and `per_page` - **no `created_time` and no `last_modified_time`** - so asset sync must be full refresh. The OAuth scope family is singular `ERP.fixedasset.*` while the path is plural `/fixedassets`, and `fixedasset` is absent from the 16 scope families printed on the OAuth page [source: https://www.zoho.com/erp/api/v3/fixed-assets/ — verified 2026-08-05].

**Design policy.**

- **Reconciliation in full, and why the anchor matters.** Every Asset Allocation in status Capitalised must map to exactly one `fixed_asset_id`, and the sum of `asset_cost` across the assets created for a CAPEX Project must equal the CWIP transferred out of that project. Because the asset carries no linkage field back to the project, PO or bill, **the Control Hub Capitalisation Request is the only anchor for this reconciliation.** If that record is lost, the trace from asset to funding CWIP is lost with it - there is no way to rebuild it from Zoho ERP.
- **Idempotency in full.** The asset list cannot be filtered by identifier or modified date, so recovering a lost `fixed_asset_id` costs a full-list scan and a fuzzy name match. Persisting the id in the same database transaction as the Asset Allocation is therefore mandatory, not a nicety.
- **Scaling caveat.** Full-refresh asset sync is comfortable at greenfield-plant volumes. At a multi-thousand-asset estate it is not, and that is recorded as a stated scaling risk rather than assumed away.

**Module facts.** API version `v3`. Base path `https://www.zohoapis.in/erp/v3`. 18 operations, 12 used by the design. Primary identifier `fixed_asset_id`. Last-modified: **None - full refresh**. Approval actions: absent. Attachments: absent. Every row below is [source: Zoho ERP OpenAPI bundle openapi-all.zip, sha256 E95A0399... — verified 2026-08-05]

### 17.7 Documented negatives - what is not in the specification

These are the rows a reader will look for and not find. They are recorded so that no implementer wastes a day searching and no reviewer mistakes an omission for an oversight. Each was established mechanically against the full bundle - 78 modules, 561 paths, 163 scopes - not by a documentation search. A documentation search cannot prove a negative; enumerating the specification can.

| # | Expected object | How it was searched | Result | What the bundle actually contains | Consequence for the build | Verified date | Verification status |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| N-01 | **Purchase Request** | All 78 modules, 561 paths and 163 scopes searched for any purchase-request, purchaserequest, requisition or PR path, module or scope | **Zero matches in all three dimensions. Proven clean negative (OAS-02)** | Nothing. The nearest objects are `purchase-order` and `purchasereceives`, which are different business objects | The Purchase Request is **owned by the Control Hub**, not by Zoho ERP. REQ-INT-001 classifies Purchase Request as a Standard Zoho ERP capability; that classification is correct at product and licensing level and is not sufficient for an API-first architecture. This is CONF-01 and it is surfaced here, not resolved | 2026-08-05 | CONFIRMED-BY-VENDOR-SPECIFICATION [source: openapi-all.zip sha256 E95A0399... — verified 2026-08-05] |
| N-02 | **Purchase-receive list or search** | `purchasereceives` module enumerated in full | **Only four operations exist (OAS-03)** | `POST /purchasereceives`, and `GET`, `PUT`, `DELETE` on `/purchasereceives/{purchasereceive_id}`. No list, no search, no filter | A receipt created in the Zoho ERP UI cannot be discovered by the Control Hub. Received-but-unbilled exposure must be reconstructed locally, and `purchasereceive_id` must be persisted at creation because it is unrecoverable afterwards | 2026-08-05 | CONFIRMED-BY-VENDOR-SPECIFICATION [source: openapi-all.zip sha256 E95A0399... — verified 2026-08-05] |
| N-03 | **Purchase-receive line to PO line link** | `purchasereceives` line schema enumerated | **No `purchaseorder_item_id` on the receipt line (OAS-03)** | Receipt lines expose only `description`, `item_id`, `item_order`, `line_item_id`, `name`, `quantity`, `unit` - and no project, tag or custom-field coding | Partial-GRN tracking against a specific PO line is not derivable from Zoho ERP. **Do not confuse this with the PO-to-bill leg, which does work** - bill lines carry `purchaseorder_item_id`, so commitment-to-actual conversion and double-count prevention are achievable against documented fields | 2026-08-05 | CONFIRMED-BY-VENDOR-SPECIFICATION [source: openapi-all.zip sha256 E95A0399... — verified 2026-08-05] |

It is a manual Zoho ERP UI prerequisite per organisation, then discovered by the connector. This becomes a blocking step in the setup wizard and a named handover item | 2026-08-05 | CONFIRMED-BY-VENDOR-SPECIFICATION [source: openapi-all.zip sha256 E95A0399... — verified 2026-08-05] |

The Control Hub Capitalisation Request is the only anchor, and if it is lost the trace is lost | 2026-08-05 | CONFIRMED-BY-VENDOR-SPECIFICATION [source: openapi-all.zip sha256 E95A0399... — verified 2026-08-05] |

Classification requires a Control Hub derivation rule, recorded as a DERIVED-RECOMMENDATION and not as a client requirement | 2026-08-05 | CONFIRMED-BY-VENDOR-SPECIFICATION [source: openapi-all.zip sha256 E95A0399... — verified 2026-08-05] |

A Zoho project can hold one roll-up number at most, and this design does not write even that, to avoid a second version of the truth | 2026-08-05 | CONFIRMED-BY-VENDOR-SPECIFICATION [source: openapi-all.zip sha256 E95A0399... — verified 2026-08-05] |
| N-09 | **Parent field on a Zoho project** | `parent` fields searched across the `projects` and `tasks` schemas | **`projects` exposes no parent field; `tasks` exposes `parent_id` (OAS-05)** | Hierarchy is available under Tasks only | The WBS Element tree is owned by the Control Hub. Mirroring it into Zoho tasks is possible but buys structure without control, so it is recorded as an option rather than a recommendation. This finding REFUTES the earlier page-scraped claim that ERP Projects offer two levels with no parent field anywhere | 2026-08-05 | CONFIRMED-BY-VENDOR-SPECIFICATION [source: openapi-all.zip sha256 E95A0399... — verified 2026-08-05] |
| N-10 | **Webhook management API** | ERP REST surface enumerated by four independent probes, including a DOM re-enumeration of the API navigation (84 unique top-level pages) and direct URL probes | **No webhooks, events, notifications, subscriptions or callbacks page exists in the ERP REST API.** Probed URLs returned HTTP 404 | Webhooks exist only as a workflow ACTION configured in the Zoho ERP UI | Webhook endpoints **cannot be provisioned as code**. They are a manual setup-wizard prerequisite, exactly like custom fields. The Control Hub must therefore treat webhooks as an accelerator and scheduled polling as the correctness guarantee - never the other way round. See section 25 API and Event Architecture | 2026-08-05 | CONFIRMED-REPLACED-QUOTE [source: https://www.zoho.com/erp/api/v3/introduction/ — verified 2026-08-05] |

Four of these ten convert what a reader would assume is configuration into build work: N-01 Purchase Request, N-02 purchase-receive listing, N-04 custom-field definitions and N-10 webhook provisioning. Any estimate produced without reading section 17.7 will be wrong, and wrong in the same direction every time.

### 17.8 Scope surface and least privilege

The bundle declares **163 distinct OAuth scopes across 44 distinct service prefixes**. The published OAuth documentation page prints **16 scope families** - contacts, settings, manufacturing, estimates, invoices, customerpayments, creditnotes, projects, expenses, salesorders, purchaseorders, bills, debitnotes, vendorpayments, banking, accountants [source: https://www.zoho.com/erp/api/v3/oauth/ — verified 2026-08-05]. Least-privilege scope design must therefore be driven from the specification, not from the documentation page (finding OAS-07). A connector built against the published 16 will request scopes it cannot use and miss scopes it needs.

Four scope irregularities in this inventory will otherwise produce a false 'missing scope' report on the connector scope-validation screen (POC step 26):

| Module | Path is | Scope family is | On the published list | Why it matters |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `fixed-assets` | plural `/fixedassets` | **singular `ERP.fixedasset.*`** | No | A validator that derives the scope from the path, or checks against the published 16, reports a granted scope as missing [source: https://www.zoho.com/erp/api/v3/fixed-assets/ — verified 2026-08-05] |
| `vendor-credits` | `/vendorcredits` | **`ERP.debitnotes.*`** | Yes, as `debitnotes` | Scope name and module name share no stem at all; a name-derived validator fails |
| `custom-modules` | `/{module_name}` | **`ERP.custommodules.ALL`** | No | An ALL-only scope with no READ variant. Enabling custom modules forfeits read-only least privilege for that surface [source: https://www.zoho.com/erp/api/v3/custom-modules/ — verified 2026-08-05] |
| `departments` | `/erp/v1/departments` | **`ERP.department.*`**, singular | No | Two irregularities on one module: an undocumented singular scope family and the v1 base path |

The complete scope-to-role mapping, the consent-screen text and the minimum scope set for the POC are specified in section 15 OAuth, Credential and Scope Management. This section supplies the raw scope strings and the irregularities only.

### 17.9 How the implementer should consume this section

1. **Load section 17.6 into the API Endpoint Catalogue entity at build time.** Each row is one catalogue record. The catalogue is data, not code, so a Zoho specification change becomes a data reload rather than a redeploy.
2. **Resolve base path from the catalogue record, never from a constant.** The `departments` module breaks a hard-coded `/erp/v3` with an unhelpful 404.
3. **Enable only the operations whose Production usage column says Used.** Every DELETE row in this inventory is explicitly not enabled, and every row marked `Not used - outside CAPEX and CWIP control` was considered and rejected rather than overlooked.
4. **Treat the Idempotency method column as mandatory, not advisory.** Two operations - `POST /purchasereceives` and `POST /fixedassets` - create records whose identifiers cannot be rediscovered through the API. A lost identifier there is a permanent data-integrity defect, not something a retry fixes.
5. **Read section 17.7 before estimating.** Four of the ten documented negatives convert apparent configuration into build work.
6. **Do not extend this inventory from memory.** If a needed operation is absent here, it is absent from the vendor specification, and the correct response is an entry marked **UNVERIFIED - REQUIRES ZOHO CONFIRMATION** in the open-questions log - not an endpoint written from recollection of a similar Zoho product.
